## A 本次交付

- 每個 Project 可在 Console 啟用內建 GitHub App plugin。
- Git 與 gh 透過 Host Unix Socket 取得短期 Token。
- 私鑰留在 Host pass；Token 只存 Host 記憶體。
- local.d 取代舊 local.json。這是不相容變更。
- 本輪依新增品質要求，將新增及修改函式複雜度控制在 15 以內。
- 自動檢查通過；真實 Linux 環境驗收仍未執行。

```callout warn 核准範圍
本頁有 6 個獨立 review 勾選框。
勾選只用於本頁核對，不儲存狀態。
全部勾選不會 push 或建立 PR。
仍需在對話中明確給予最終核准。
```

完整版本：`34b3414f2451e2e713a0880d3f533bd48e5b4614` → `0fecc5ec14dec722b8952476a8a223a301e3eadd`。

完整實作 diff 排除生成的審查頁與 diff 本身。

[完整實作 diff](2026-10-24-project-plugins-changes.diff) · [已核准規格](2026-10-24-project-plugins-spec.html)

## B 架構與變更地圖

```flow LR
+ G1 local.d -> 合併設定: 排序與 deep merge
+ G2 plugin manager -> G4 Host Socket: systemd user service
+ G3 provider -> pass 與 GitHub: JWT 與單一 repo Token
+ G4 Host Socket -> G3 provider: 按需取得與快取
+ G5 Git 與 gh adapter -> G4 Host Socket: HTTP over Unix Socket
+ G6 Console -> G2 plugin manager: 設定與生命週期
+ G6 Console -> 合併設定: Start Build Connect
~ 合併設定
```

G1–G6 對應下方直接展開的變更群組。
G7 是測試；G8 是文件。
「新增／修改」指群組內包含兩類檔案。

左側是原始版本；右側是修改後版本。
紅色表示刪除；綠色表示新增。

窄視窗可在每個對照區塊內水平捲動。
節錄與完整檔案均取自指定 Git 版本。
其餘變更請由索引查閱[完整 diff](2026-10-24-project-plugins-changes.diff)。

### G2 新增／修改：Project plugin 管理

確認每個 Project 獨立設定與服務。摘要比對保護手改檔；失敗保留可重試狀態。

需你 review：是。


### G3 新增：GitHub App provider

確認單一 repository 與選定權限。先用 App JWT 驗證 installation，再申請 Token。

需你 review：是。

```html
<label><input type="checkbox" id="check-G3"> 我已確認 G3 的決策與風險</label>
```

### G4 新增：Host Unix Socket

確認信任邊界：可連 Socket 的程序可取 Token。目錄 0700、Socket 0600；不開 TCP。

需你 review：是。


Show command help</code></td><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true">55</td><td class="dch-new" data-new-line="55"><code>dch help                  # Show command help</code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true">52</td><td class="dch-old" data-old-line="52"><code>```</code></td><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true">56</td><td class="dch-new" data-new-line="56"><code>```</code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true">53</td><td class="dch-old" data-old-line="53"><code></code></td><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true">57</td><td class="dch-new" data-new-line="57"><code></code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true">54</td><td class="dch-old" data-old-line="54"><code>### Configuration file</code></td><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true">58</td><td class="dch-new" data-new-line="58"><code>### Configuration file</code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true">55</td><td class="dch-old" data-old-line="55"><code></code></td><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true">59</td><td class="dch-new" data-new-line="59"><code></code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number dch-deletion" aria-hidden="true">56</td><td class="dch-old dch-deletion" data-old-line="56"><code>`~/.dch/config` is JSON without a `.json` extension. It stores **only project paths**. dch reads each project&#x27;s name from the merged `.devcontainer/devcontainer.json` and optional `.devcontainer/devcontainer.local.json`; comments and trailing commas are supported in both. If `name` is missing, the directory name is used.</code></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">60</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="60"><code>`~/.dch/config` is JSON without a `.json` extension. It stores registered project paths and enabled plugin settings. Private keys, JWTs, and access tokens are not stored there. dch reads each project&#x27;s name from the merged `.devcontainer/devcontainer.json` and optional first-level `.devcontainer/devcontainer.local.d/*.json` fragments; comments and trailing commas are supported. If `name` is missing, the directory name is used.</code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true">57</td><td class="dch-old" data-old-line="57"><code></code></td><td [</code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true">62</td><td class="dch-old" data-old-line="62"><code>    &quot;/home/user/projects/app-one&quot;,</code></td><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true">66</td><td class="dch-new" data-new-line="66"><code>    &quot;/home/user/projects/app-one&quot;,</code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true">63</td><td class="dch-old" data-old-line="63"><code>    &quot;/home/user/projects/app-two&quot;</code></td><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true">67</td><td class="dch-new" class="dch-old" data-old-line="65"><code>}</code></td><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true">69</td><td class="dch-new" data-new-line="69"><code>}</code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true">66</td><td class="dch-old" data-old-line="66"><code>```</code></td><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true">70</td><td class="dch-new" data-new-line="70"><code>```</code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">71</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="71"><code></code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">72</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="72"><code>### GitHub App plugin</code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">73</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="73"><code></code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">74</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="74"><code>The built-in GitHub App plugin is Linux-only and gives one project access to one selected GitHub repository. It requires local Docker, a systemd user service, `pass`, and a GPG agent on the host. Create and install the GitHub App, then store its RSA private key in the host&#x27;s `pass` store. The key entry must be readable by the host user running the dch systemd user service.</code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">75</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="75"><code></code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">76</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="76"><code>Before enabling the plugin, unlock the host GPG agent without printing the key:</code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">77</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="77"><code></code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">78</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="78"><code>```sh</code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">79</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="79"><code>pass show &lt;entry&gt; &gt;/dev/null</code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">80</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="80"><code>```</code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">81</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="81"><code></code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">82</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="82"><code>The GPG agent must be able to decrypt without opening a prompt. dch invokes `pass` non-interactively; if the GPG agent&#x27;s cached unlock expires, unlock the entry again before the next token refresh. The private key remains on the host and is never mounted into the container.</code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">83</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="83"><code></code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">84</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="84"><code>Open **Project → Plugins** to configure and enable the plugin. Enter the App ID, installation ID, pass entry, repository (`owner/repository`), and choose `read`, `write`, or disabled for `contents` and `pull_requests`. dch verifies the service and credential before enabling. The resulting installation token is limited to that repository and selected permissions.</code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">85</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="85"><code></code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">86</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="86"><code>The container needs Git 2.30 or newer, GitHub CLI (`gh`), curl, and POSIX `sh`. The plugin adds a read-only mount at `/run/dch-github`, a Git credential helper for HTTPS `github.com`, and a `gh` wrapper available from Connect shells. The wrapper requests a token for each invocation and sets `GH_TOKEN` only for that `gh` process. Other Git hosts keep their existing credential helpers; SSH URLs are unchanged. Existing lifecycle hooks are preserved. The socket directory is mode `0700` and the socket is mode `0600`; the container user must be able to connect to it, so a mismatched UID will fail.</code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">87</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="87"><code></code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">88</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="88"><code>Any container process that can access the socket can request that project&#x27;s short-lived token, and Docker administrators can access the mount. Use the plugin only with containers you trust.</code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">89</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="89"><code></code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">90</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="90"><code>After enabling, Build the project to apply the mount and initialize the Git helper. Configuration changes and disabling the plugin also mark an existing container as needing Build. dch does not rebuild automatically. Start and Connect are blocked for an existing container until Build succeeds. Disabling stops the host service and removes its runtime files immediately; removing a project also disables its plugin before unregistering it.</code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">91</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="91"><code></code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">92</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="92"><code>The systemd user service stays active when dch closes or the container stops. Tokens are kept in host memory only. The service reuses a token while it has more than five minutes left and refreshes it on the next credential request when five minutes or less remain. It does not write token files or run a refresh timer.</code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">93</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="93"><code></code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">94</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="94"><code>To check that the host service is alive without requesting a credential, use:</code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">95</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="95"><code></code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">96</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="96"><code>```sh</code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">97</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="97"><code>curl --fail --silent --show-error --max-time 5 --unix-socket &quot;$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR/dch/&lt;project-hash&gt;/credential.sock&quot; http://localhost/health</code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">98</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="98"><code>```</code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">99</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="99"><code></code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">100</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="100"><code>The health endpoint returns only service status. Do not request or print `/credential` when checking service health.</code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">101</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="101"><code></code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">102</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="102"><code>#### Manual Linux verification</code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">103</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="103"><code></code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">104</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="104"><code>1. In **Project → Plugins**, enable the GitHub App and confirm dch verifies the service and credential without printing the token.</code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">105</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="105"><code>2. Close dch and stop the container. Confirm the user service remains active; run the `/health` request above and expect `ok`.</code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">106</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="106"><code>3. Build and Connect. Confirm Git fetch/push and `gh pr create` use the configured repository and expected GitHub identity and permissions.</code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">107</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="107"><code>4. Restart the user service while the container is running, then use Git or `gh` again to confirm the mounted runtime directory exposes the replacement socket.</code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">108</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="108"><code>5. Change the plugin settings or disable it. Confirm the existing container shows **Build required**, Start and Connect are blocked, and disabling stops the service immediately. Build to apply or remove the mount.</code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">109</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="109"><code>6. Repeat with a container user whose UID does not match the host socket owner and confirm connection fails. Also try enabling while the GPG agent cannot decrypt non-interactively and confirm activation fails without exposing key or token contents.</code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">110</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="110"><code></code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">111</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="111"><code>These checks require Linux, Docker, systemd, `pass`, and GPG agent access; they are manual integration checks.</code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">112</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="112"><code></code></td></tr><tr><td class="dch-line-number" aria-hidden="true"></td><td class="dch-old dch-empty"></td><td class="dch-line-number dch-addition" aria-hidden="true">113</td><td class="dch-new dch-addition" data-new-line="113"><code>Project-specific settings belong in `.devcontainer/devcontainer.local.d/` as first-level `.json` files. dch merges them in filename order; keep local fragments out of version control when they contain machine-specific settings. dch no longer reads `.devcontainer/devcontainer.local.json`; existing files are ignored and never converted automatically.</code></td></tr></tbody></table></div></details>

## D 使用行為與相容性

| 面向 | 原行為 | 最終行為 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 個人容器設定 | 讀取 devcontainer.local.json | 只讀 local.d 第一層正常 .json 檔 |
| 合併 | 單一 local 檔 | 檔名排序；JSONC；object 深合併；array 追加去重 |
| 認證 | 使用者自備 | Console 驗證服務與 credential 後啟用 |
| 設定變更 | 無 plugin 狀態 | 既有容器標記 Build required；阻擋 Start／Connect |
| 停用 | 無 plugin 服務 | 停止服務並清理；容器設定需 Build 才恢復 |
| 移除 Project | 取消註冊 | 先清理 plugin；失敗保留設定供重試 |
| Git | 使用既有 helper | 僅 HTTPS github.com 走 plugin；其他 Host 保留原 helper |
| gh | 原有命令 | wrapper 每次取憑證；只對該子程序設定 GH_TOKEN |

```callout warn Breaking change：舊 local.json 使用者
舊檔會被靜默忽略，不提示也不自動搬移。
若要保留設定，請手動搬到 local.d 的 .json 片段。
例如：`.devcontainer/devcontainer.local.d/90-user.json`。
請自行將個人片段加入版本控制忽略規則。
```

新建容器可由 Start 套用設定。
既有容器需 Build，且保留資料移除確認。
服務在關閉 dch 或 Stop 容器後保留。
停用不會撤銷已回傳的短期 Token。

## E 認證與信任邊界

```sequence num
Git 或 gh -> Socket: GET /credential
Socket -> Provider: 請求 Token
Provider -> Provider: 檢查記憶體快取
Provider -> pass: 需要更新才讀取私鑰
Provider -> GitHub: App JWT 驗證 repo installation
GitHub --> Provider: installation ID 必須符合設定
Provider -> GitHub: 申請單一 repo 與選定權限
Provider --> Socket: 短期 Token
Socket --> Git 或 gh: no-store 回應
```

快取剩餘時間超過五分鐘時直接重用。
剩餘五分鐘以下時，由下一次請求更新。
並行請求共用一次更新；沒有 timer 或 Token File。

| 邊界 | 限制 |
| --- | --- |
| Host | Linux、本機 Docker、systemd user service、pass／GPG |
| 容器 | image／Dockerfile；Git 2.30+、gh、curl、POSIX sh |
| Socket | 目前使用者擁有的 XDG_RUNTIME_DIR；目錄 0700、Socket 0600 |
| 掛載 | 整個 runtime 目錄唯讀；服務重啟後可看見新 Socket |
| 信任 | 可連 Socket 的程序可取得該 Project Token；Docker 管理員亦可存取 |
| 不支援 | Compose、遠端 Docker、GitHub Enterprise、SSH 認證、多 repo Token |

容器 UID 必須能連線 Socket；錯誤訊息提示檢查 UID。
此環境未驗證真實容器 UID 與重啟行為。
